function changeComboQty(id,delta){
  const p=comboProducts[id];
  p.qty=Math.max(0,p.qty+delta);
  document.getElementById('qty-'+id).textContent=p.qty;
  document.getElementById('sub-'+id).textContent=(p.qty*p.price).toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
  refreshComboSummary();
}

function refreshComboSummary(){
  let selected=0,totalQty=0,total=0,saved=0;
  Object.entries(comboProducts).forEach(([id,p])=>{
    if(p.qty>0) selected++;
    totalQty+=p.qty;
    total+=p.qty*p.price;

    if(p.qty>0){
      const qtyEl=document.getElementById('qty-'+id);
      const row=qtyEl ? qtyEl.closest('.product-row') : null;
      const mrpEl=row ? row.querySelector('.mrp-price') : null;
      if(mrpEl){
        const mrp=parseFloat((mrpEl.textContent||'').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||0;
        if(mrp>p.price) saved+=(mrp-p.price)*p.qty;
      }
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('selectedProducts').textContent=selected;
  document.getElementById('totalQty').textContent=totalQty;
  document.getElementById('sampleNetTotal').textContent=total.toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
  document.getElementById('samplePayBtn').disabled=totalQty===0;

  const lp=document.getElementById('liveProducts');
  const li=document.getElementById('liveItems');
  const la=document.getElementById('liveAmount');
  const ls=document.getElementById('liveSave');
  if(lp) lp.textContent=selected;
  if(li) li.textContent=totalQty;
  if(la) la.textContent=total.toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
  if(ls) ls.textContent=saved.toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
}

function productImageError(img){
  if(!img || img.dataset.fallbackApplied==='1') return;
  img.dataset.fallbackApplied='1';
  img.src='images/crackers/product-image-fallback.svg';
  img.dataset.full='';
  img.classList.add('image-fallback');
  img.title='Product image';
}
function normalizeCatalogueName(s){
  return String(s||'').toUpperCase()
    .replace(/&QUOT;/g,'"').replace(/&AMP;/g,'&')
    .replace(/[“”]/g,'"').replace(/[’]/g,"'")
    .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
}
async function hydrateCatalogueImages(){
  const targets=[...document.querySelectorAll('.product-row img[data-source-product]')];
  if(!targets.length) return;
  try{
    const res=await fetch('/api/catalog-images',{headers:{'Accept':'application/json'}});
    if(!res.ok) return;
    const data=await res.json();
    const items=Array.isArray(data.items)?data.items:[];
    for(const img of targets){
      const key=normalizeCatalogueName(img.dataset.sourceProduct||img.alt);
      const match=items.find(x=>x&&x.key&&(x.key.includes(key)||key.includes(x.key)));
      if(match&&match.src){
        img.dataset.fallbackApplied='';
        img.classList.remove('image-fallback');
        img.src=match.src;
        img.dataset.full=getFullProductImage(match.src);
        img.title='Click to enlarge';
      }
    }
  }catch(err){}
}
function getFullProductImage(src){
  const marker='/plain/';
  if(src && src.includes(marker)){
    try{return decodeURIComponent(src.substring(src.indexOf(marker)+marker.length));}catch(err){}
  }
  return src;
}
function openProductImage(src,alt,fullSrc){
  if(!fullSrc && String(src||'').includes('product-image-fallback.svg')) return;
  const modal=document.getElementById('productImageModal');
  const inner=modal.querySelector('.img-modal-inner');
  const large=document.getElementById('productImageLarge');
  large.classList.remove('zoom2x');
  inner.classList.remove('is-zoomed','dragging');
  inner.scrollLeft=0; inner.scrollTop=0;
  large.src=fullSrc||getFullProductImage(src);
  large.alt=alt||'Cracker product image';
  modal.classList.add('open');
  document.body.style.overflow='hidden';
}
function setProductZoom(zoomIn,e){
  if(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
  const modal=document.getElementById('productImageModal');
  const inner=modal.querySelector('.img-modal-inner');
  const large=document.getElementById('productImageLarge');
  large.classList.toggle('zoom2x',!!zoomIn);
  inner.classList.toggle('is-zoomed',!!zoomIn);
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
    if(zoomIn){
      inner.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,(inner.scrollWidth-inner.clientWidth)/2);
      inner.scrollTop=Math.max(0,(inner.scrollHeight-inner.clientHeight)/2);
    }else{
      inner.scrollLeft=0; inner.scrollTop=0;
    }
  });
}
function toggleProductZoom(e){
  if(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
  const large=document.getElementById('productImageLarge');
  setProductZoom(!large.classList.contains('zoom2x'),e);
}
function closeProductImage(e){
  if(e && (e.target.id==='productImageLarge' || e.target.closest?.('.zoom-tools'))) return;
  const modal=document.getElementById('productImageModal');
  const inner=modal.querySelector('.img-modal-inner');
  modal.classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('productImageLarge').classList.remove('zoom2x');
  inner.classList.remove('is-zoomed','dragging');
  document.body.style.overflow='';
}
let imageDrag={active:false,x:0,y:0,left:0,top:0};
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  // Add S.No header to every category and number all products continuously.
  document.querySelectorAll('.catalog-head').forEach(head=>{
    if(!head.querySelector('.head-serial')){
      const cell=document.createElement('div');
      cell.className='head-serial';
      cell.textContent='S.NO';
      head.prepend(cell);
    }
  });
  let serialNo=1;
  document.querySelectorAll('.product-row').forEach(row=>{
    if(!row.querySelector(':scope > .serial-col')){
      const cell=document.createElement('div');
      cell.className='serial-col';
      cell.textContent=serialNo;
      row.prepend(cell);
    }
    serialNo++;
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.product-row img').forEach(img=>{
    img.addEventListener('error',()=>productImageError(img));
    if(img.complete && img.naturalWidth===0) productImageError(img);
  });
  refreshComboSummary();
  hydrateCatalogueImages();
  document.querySelectorAll('.product-row img').forEach(img=>img.setAttribute('title','Click to enlarge'));
  const modal=document.getElementById('productImageModal');
  const inner=modal.querySelector('.img-modal-inner');
  const large=document.getElementById('productImageLarge');
  inner.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
    if(!large.classList.contains('zoom2x') || e.target.closest('.zoom-tools') || e.target.closest('.img-modal-close')) return;
    imageDrag={active:true,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,left:inner.scrollLeft,top:inner.scrollTop};
    inner.classList.add('dragging');
    e.preventDefault();
  });
  window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
    if(!imageDrag.active) return;
    inner.scrollLeft=imageDrag.left-(e.clientX-imageDrag.x);
    inner.scrollTop=imageDrag.top-(e.clientY-imageDrag.y);
  });
  window.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>{
    imageDrag.active=false;
    inner.classList.remove('dragging');
  });
});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape') closeProductImage();});

function comboReadyToPay(){
  const chosen=Object.values(comboProducts).filter(p=>p.qty>0);
  if(!chosen.length) return;
  let total=0;
  const lines=['DEEPAVALI CRACKERS 2026 – READY TO PAY',''];
  chosen.forEach((p,i)=>{
    const sub=p.qty*p.price;
    total+=sub;
    lines.push((i+1)+'. '+p.name);
    lines.push('Qty: '+p.qty+' | Unit Price: ₹'+p.price.toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2})+' | Sub Total: ₹'+sub.toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}));
    lines.push('');
  });
  lines.push('NET PRODUCT TOTAL: ₹'+total.toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}));
  lines.push('Packing & Shipping Charges Extra');
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('I am ready to make payment. Please confirm availability and send payment details.');
  window.open('https://wa.me/919488917786?text='+encodeURIComponent(lines.join('\n')),'_blank');
}
</script>
</body><!-- deployment-refresh -->
</html>
<!-- summary-dashboard-deploy-refresh -->
